\n\t\t\t\t\tდირექტორის თანაშემწე \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t \n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tსამშენებლო კომპანია\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t03 სექტემბერი03 ოქტომბერი\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t
